  - **The real finding is stronger than the filed one: of 185 skeletons created, 175 (94.6%) were discarded here and 169 — 91.4% of every skeleton ever created — had provably removed the diagnostic they targeted.** And **80% of skeleton-bearing chains end by running out of proposals**, only 11% on the no-progress rule the ticket's counter-argument described. What was thrown away: **3.6 types and 3.6 re-derivations per run** (run `01-10-45`: 12 and 18). That is what was discarded, NOT a predicted outcome.
- **T2-EZ's field DoD was unmet after two runs (met in run 3, above), and the reason is checked rather than assumed.** ~270 cycles, **zero skeleton creations**, because both runs' generator wrote code that compiled (ONE failing probe each, against a corpus where 62.6% of rejections are compile failures). Only **46% of archived runs create a skeleton at all**, so this is NO-OPPORTUNITY. **What IS field-proven:** the readout is Spring-wired, renders, and lands `injector_files_created/_kept/_rederived` in `summary.txt`; and in run 2 the **chain-stopped-red branch actually executed** and correctly preserved nothing.
  - **The "created, not edited" condition earned its place in the field.** `LoanService.java` had `Book` and `Member` unimported; `symbol-import:local` resolved both and each cleared its diagnostic — but the same file also carried `incompatible types`, so the chain stopped and both correct fixes rolled back. **Preserving them would have been wrong**: they edit a file that must not land. A rule scoped by "did the fix work?" alone would have merged a type-broken service by the back door.
  - **⚠ `injector_fixes_rederived` spans EVERY injector rule, not just creating ones**, so it is a superset of what preservation can address and will not reach zero. Do not compare it against `kept` as if they shared a denominator.
  - **⚠ Expect `repeats_refused` to FALL.** Preserved files move the tree fingerprint T2-EY scopes its results to, so more re-proposals are legitimately let through. No code needed — T2-EY chose derivation over announcement for exactly this.
- **🔴 T2-FB FILED — A LIVELOCK THAT ENDED A RUN, and it is the veto-versus-actuator problem with a price tag.** `T3-BL: all criteria banked → FULFILLED` immediately followed by `T4-CU: not promoting — its own acceptance criteria are contradicted by the running artifact`. Criteria stay banked, so it repeats: **78 early exits on ONE subgoal, 103 FULFILLED applications, 60 of the last 60 cycles ending `criteria_banked_early_exit`.** Both halves behave correctly; nothing converts the refusal into WORK. **Measured cost: 148 package-gate and 147 boot-gate runs against 26 each in the same day's other run — ~16 minutes of a 50-minute run** — and `T4-I` finally halted it, `verify-before-pause found nothing to promote` (T4-CU's refusal blocking the escape hatch too). Cheapest fix: a subgoal whose promotion was refused must not re-take the early exit unchanged.
- **✅ T2-FA BUILT — the symbol path now defers to a package migration that can actually clear the build.** Second causal trace confirmed first, as the ticket required (run `20-38-08` 21:10, a different controller: two symbol passes red with `package javax.validation does not exist` in EVERY re-probe, then the prefix rewrite green). **Nothing new was built — both halves existed and only the ORDER was wrong**; the deferral reads the package diagnostic from the same stderr via the classifier's own `extractToken` and routes to the existing `resolveMissingPackage`. Narrow by construction: needs a package diagnostic AND a registered replacement, so a package with no successor falls through and legacy projects are already excluded. The test is the field's own stderr and asserts the DEAD import is gone, not merely that a proposal appeared.
- **✅ T6-K FIELD-CONFIRMED ON FIRST USE, and its first reading refines the ticket.** Run `22-15-16` (**GOAL_FULFILLED, 20/23, 10.7 min, 15 cycles**) recorded `smoke_failed_checks=14,15,20` with the FAIL lines preserved. **Checks 14 and 15 are two of the five spurious-pass candidates — and here they FAILED** (`want 404 got 400`), because the endpoint exists and answers the wrong status. **So the spurious pass is a property of the ARTIFACT STATE, not of the check**, which is what the deferred gating rule must key on.
- **⚠ A DEFECT OF MINE, found in the field in output I had just shipped: an empty counter was being stored as `""`.** preflight writes several counters unconditionally, so a run whose mechanism never fired emits `injector_files_created=`; the ledger tested `if k in summary` and stored the empty string — neither absent nor a number, and read for truthiness it looks like zero, **the exact "unknown read as zero" every one of those field comments warns against, including ones I wrote hours earlier.** Fixed where the row is built so it covers every field at once, including T2-EY's, which had the hole first. One polluted row cleaned.
- **✅ T6-K's PRESERVATION HALF SHIPPED — the failing check SET is now recorded.** `build_boot_smoke` piped smoke through `tail -1` and discarded the 23 check lines, which made the standing "compare sets, never totals" rule unfollowable after the fact. Now `<tag>-smoke.txt` in the run dir, `smoke_failed_checks=10,19` in `summary.txt`, carried by the ledger, **absent rather than empty** so "nothing failed" and "not measured" stay distinct. **Changes no score and re-bases nothing** — the gating-rule half that would stays open and unbuilt.
- **T2-FA's original filing — the injector's most-fired path fails 77% and burns the recurrence budget.** `symbol-import` on the jakarta family: **111 fires, 85 red, across 32 runs**; `package-replace` goes green 28 of 34. The class-symbol path adds a `jakarta` import beside the file's still-broken `javax` one — it cannot compile by construction — and each failure counts toward T2-EN's cap, which reached **worst 5x against a cap of 6**. The real cost is a false escalation, not the compile.
- **🔴 T6-K FILED — five smoke checks, not two, can be passed by the endpoint being ABSENT.** Every expected-404 check has the property; only the loans pair showed it in the run that was examined. Filed rather than fixed: it re-bases a yardstick 45 runs of ledger are scored on, and would WIDEN the spread rather than shift it. **Also found: the failing check SET is not preserved anywhere** — only the total — which makes [[project-smoke-score-is-chained]]'s "compare sets, never totals" unfollowable. Two-line fix, and a precondition for any re-scoring.
- **✅ T2-ET's OWN "cheapest first move" DONE OFFLINE, no code.** Over 37 preserved run trees: 1488 goals, **692 SUBSUMED (46.5%)**; of 52 observation-raised items, 19 subsumed — **10 decomposed, 9 leaves with nothing carrying them**. The frozen-series figures reproduce (3 and 2, including the ticket's own note that one was legitimately decomposed); the third gives 0 against its 2 and the discrepancy is left standing. **Per-run rate ~0.24, far below the frozen series' 3·2·2** — those runs were observation-heavy. The share travels; the rate does not.
- **⚠ TWO OF MY OWN DEFECTS, both caught before they mattered:** a test that passed WITHOUT exercising its path (overlapping Mockito `contains(...)` matchers shadowed the second stub, so only one skeleton was ever created) — rewritten with sequenced returns; and a claim about how chains terminate asserted from ONE example, then measured (80/11/9). Also a cost estimate off by 6x, corrected from the run's own readout.
- **✅ BOTH FIELD DoDs MET IN RUN 3 (`2026-08-01_20-38-08`), minutes apart, and both verified consequence by consequence.** T2-EZ preserved **two** injector-created types on red builds — `BookService` at 20:42 and `BusinessRuleException` at 21:31, the exception class the ticket was filed about — with run totals **2 created, 2 kept, 0 re-derived, 0 discarded**, both on disk, each created exactly once. T2-FB cleared 3 banked criterion reads seven minutes after shipping, and **the loop then ran an agent on that subgoal instead of re-declaring it FULFILLED** (1 early exit and 1 refusal, against 78 in run 2's version of the same shape).
- **Runs:** `18-36-18` deadline, 49 cycles, 24 merges, smoke 16/23 · `19-29-42` honest-halt:root-convergence-stall, 176 cycles, 25 merges, smoke 21/23 · `20-38-08` deadline, 54 cycles, 17 merges, 59 min, smoke 15/23, peaked at its final cycle. **⚠ 16 / 21 / 15 IS NOT A TREND AND MUST NOT BE READ AS ONE** — the failing check SETS are preserved nowhere (T6-K), the score is a chain not 23 measurements ([[project-smoke-score-is-chained]]), and three runs under measured variance cannot show an outcome effect for a mechanism that fired twice. **Neither T2-EZ nor T2-FB has demonstrated an outcome effect, and neither is claimed to have one** — what is demonstrated is that each does the thing it was built to do.
- **STATE:** suite **2426/0/5**, **20 commits this session, none pushed**, no processes running, nothing in flight.
- **✅ T2-FB BUILT AND CLOSED — one line, in the home the invariant already had, after two wrong estimates.** The refusal now drops the refuted claim's banked pass-map, so `allCriteriaDefinedAndBanked` reads false and T3-BL yields. **The rationale was already written in this codebase twice** — the closure-edge reopen path clears the same map for the same stated reason, and T4-CD's resume re-grounding treats a loaded bank as hypothesis. T4-CU's refusal was the one contradiction path missing it. Suite **2424/0/5**; field marker is `T2-FB: cleared N banked criterion read(s)`.
- **⚠ THE TWO WRONG ESTIMATES ARE THE LESSON, not the fix.** (1) A metadata flag blocking the early exit — built, compiled, **reverted**: a second mechanism for an invariant that has a home ([[feedback-one-invariant-one-home]]). (2) Widening `ClosureClaimVerifier` to name the refuted criterion — abandoned on inspection: the contradiction forms inside `BootGate.launchAndObserve` and surfaces through `verifyBootsAndServes`, **which the completion-edge gate also calls**, so it would have changed the return type of the most safety-critical gate in the system to fix a livelock. Reading the prior art before the third attempt is what made it one line. I implemented the obvious flag (block the early exit until a merge moves past the refusal) and it compiled and would have worked. But this loop already has a home: the run-20 fix answers the identical failure by CORRECTING THE BANK so `allCriteriaDefinedAndBanked` goes false and T3-BL yields by itself — its test says *"corrected, not just bypassed"*. A second bypassing flag is the two-patches-one-invariant shape [[feedback-one-invariant-one-home]] forbids. **The right fix needs `ClosureClaimVerifier.refute` widened to name the refuted CRITERION** (text, not index — the implementation filters to executable criteria and loses the mapping), carried on `RefusedClaimRefuted`, then unbanked through the existing path. Five files, one mechanism. It is also strictly better: unbanking makes the loop RE-CHECK, so the repair has something to aim at, where the flag only stopped the claim being re-made.
- **NEXT, in order:** ① field-confirm T2-FA (`T2-FA: deferring the missing-symbol fix`) and T6-K (`smoke_failed_checks` in summary.txt) — run 4 `2026-08-01_22-15-16` is in flight for exactly this ② T2-EZ's field DoD needs a run whose generator writes code that does not compile; it will come, and the counters accumulate by themselves ③ **T2-FA**'s second causal trace (run 2 showed the SHAPE with a different rule, not the jakarta trace) ④ T6-K's two-line check-set preservation, which every later comparison depends on.

## 2026-08-01 (Opus 5, user-directed → autonomous) — THE LOOP'S CONSTRAINT IS ACTING ON WHAT IT ALREADY KNOWS, NOW WITH TWO ACTUATORS AND A RULE

**The user asked why 261 `docs` commits against 111 `feat` had produced 2 perfect runs in 45, and directed that the three fixes named in the answer be built in one session.** All three are in, plus a measured refutation and two self-caught wiring defects.

- **The composition that prompted it, measured:** 261 `docs` / 111 `feat` / 74 `fix` since 2026-07-01; file touches since 07-25 are 180 memory-doc, 115 prod-Java, 99 script, 69 test. The codebase carries **84 files under `analysis/` and 5 under `observability/` against 33 agent classes**, plus 28 offline Python instruments. Outcomes over the full ledger: **21 of 45 runs scorable, mean 16.5/23, two 23/23 ever (07-24 and 07-28), no trend.**
- **✅ T2-EY BUILT AND CLOSED — a proposal this run already rejected is refused before the probe and before the evaluator.** T4-BX's relay demonstrably delivers the rejection reason into the next prompt; nothing made it a constraint on what the next proposal may CONTAIN. Measured over **33 run corpora**: 952 proposals / 438 rejections / 502 merges in 1530 cycles; **39 rejections are the same content rejected twice, in 15 of the 33 runs.** Escalation is graded — one subgoal → DIVERGENCE_REQUEST; survived a change of subgoal → REPLAN_REQUESTED, on the SECOND occurrence rather than the tenth.
  - **The safety condition was WRONG on the first cut and re-measurement caught it.** "Stands until the next merge" assumes a merge is the only writer; the compile-fix injector's files are deliberately preserved through the rejection rollback (T4-AQ), so **38 of 39 have no intervening merge but only 34 have no intervening injector write** — four wrong refusals. The result is now **scoped to a fingerprint of the tree** (`previousOutput()`), so it expires by itself whichever writer moved the tree and nobody enumerates writers.
- **✅ T4-CU's PLACEMENT GAP CLOSED — criteria are executed when the SUBGOAL closes, not only at the run's closure edge.** The capability existed; nothing probed a work item's criteria at the moment it was marked done. Built as a **port** (`goal/ClosureClaimVerifier` ← `core/build/BehaviouralClosureVerifier`) so the goal tree does not learn what an endpoint is — **T4-DD's first real exercise**, and the answer the earlier static-check deferral was waiting for. It **refutes and never confirms**: 150 of 452 closed subgoals across 32 corpora (33%) carry an executable criterion, so blocking on abstention would stall two thirds of all work items. Cost ~5 firings/run at package 3.0s + boot 2.6s — under 30s of 90 minutes.
- **✅ THE RULE BOTH WERE INSTANCES OF — [[feedback-instrument-needs-an-actuator]].** An observability ticket ships its actuator in the SAME scope; **prose relayed into another agent's prompt is not an actuator** — it is context the generator may ignore, and measurably does. Readout-first must be argued for and carry the condition that wires it. Distinct from [[feedback-ship-full-scope]], which is about migration debt; this is a different half-ticket.
- **🔴 THE BIGGEST BUCKET HAS NO OWNER, AND THE OBVIOUS FIX IS REFUTED — [[project-rejection-composition]].** **62.6% of all rejections are compile failures**, and 52.3% of those are `REFUSED_AUTHOR_ERROR` — the model writing code that does not type-check against symbols that already exist (~a third of all rejections). The hypothesis "the generator cannot see the signatures it calls" is **wrong**: `STRUCTURE_SUMMARY` fires on **91 of 91** evolution prompts carrying whole declaration lines with parameter types, confirmed against a rendered prompt. Do not file it.
- **⚠ TWO OF MY OWN WIRING DEFECTS, both caught before the field, both the session's own recurring shape:**
  - **T4-CU was placed on the path the loop does not take.** `transitionGoal(COMPLETED)` is not how a fulfilled subgoal closes — `promoteCurrentSubGoal` calls `markGoalCompleted` **directly**. Moved to `markGoalCompleted`, the chokepoint both paths reach. Found by tracing the call graph; **the tests passed either way**, so they now assert the promotion path by name.
  - **T2-EY's unit tests were passing vacuously.** A bare Mockito mock of `EvaluationInterpreter.InterpretationResult` answers `null` for `mutationState()`, so the agent never recorded a rejection — **nine green tests against a gate that had never fired once**. Rewritten as a round trip through the agent; five failed immediately, which is what a real assertion looks like.
- **⚠ PROCESS: [[feedback-no-heavy-mvn-beside-a-live-run]].** The scratch-`FILE_BASE_PATH` unlock covers live LOGS, not resource contention. A full suite beside a live run took **load to 159 and available memory to 0**; Neo4j then failed every connection and the run died at 10 cycles. Ports stayed bound and the container reported `Up` throughout — `/proc/loadavg` was the only honest signal.
- **✅ BOTH MECHANISMS FIRED IN THE FIELD, in full.** Run `2026-08-01_00-23-26`: T2-EY refused a repeat with no probe and no evaluator call, reason relayed verbatim. Run `2026-08-01_01-10-45`: **13 refusals** (one fingerprint 7× across 2 subgoals, exercising the REPLAN escalation for the first time) and **T4-CU refused two closures** whose criteria the running artifact contradicts — both the `POST /members` validation-not-enforced shape, the same class that starved a repair on 2026-07-30.
- **🔴 AND NEITHER MOVED THE OUTCOME — the honest result.** The user's framing was "stop building observers, build actuators". **I built two VETOES and called them actuators.** A veto changes the record; an actuator changes the work. Measurements: one fingerprint refused 7× and the loop kept emitting it; **both refused closures ended `SUBSUMED — superseded by replanning`**, i.e. `T2-ET`'s open defect consumed T4-CU's output. Moving the probe to the leaf moved the leak one step later. **T2-ET is the binding ticket for that chain now, not T4-CU.**
- **🔴 THE SMOKE SCORE IS A CHAIN — read [[project-smoke-score-is-chained]] BEFORE comparing any two scores.** Booted run `01-10-45`'s artifact and ran the suite per check: 14/23 is **8 failures from ONE missing file** (`LoanController.java`), 1 independent validation gap, and **2 checks that PASS SPURIOUSLY** because they want 404 for a bad id and the absent endpoint 404s everything. Scores cluster at 10/14/18/23 because those are chain-link states. **I compared 18→14 across builds as if it were a regression; that is a category error and it is now written down so it is not repeated.**
- **➡️ THE NEXT TICKET, root-caused and filed with measurements: `T2-EZ`.** The 14/23 traces to a closed valve. `LoanController` was proposed 8×, merged 0×, 7 deaths on the compile probe; the errors are **missing TYPES** (575 `cannot find symbol` — `Loan` 55, `LoanService` 26, `BookRepository` 93, plus 8 exception classes the model references and never creates, inventing variant names between attempts). The injector fires hard — **`skeleton-class token=Loan` 138 times** — and the final artifact holds **one** exception class. Cause: `CompileProbeService`'s chain-stopped restore rolls back with **no preserved-paths argument** while `EvaluationAgent`'s T4-AQ carve-out preserves injector files only on the **COMPILE_OK** path. **Skeletons survive a green build and are discarded on a red one** — backwards from where they are needed. Measurement first (same-token re-creation counts over the corpora); the counter-argument is on the ticket.
- **STATE:** suite **2410/0/5**, bookkeeping clean (0 warnings, 438 tickets), **28 commits this session, none pushed**, no processes running.
- **NEXT, in order:** ① `T2-EZ`'s measurement, then the preserve-skeletons decision — the only actuator-shaped item with a measured 8-point payoff behind it. ② `T2-ET`, without which T4-CU's refusals keep being replanned away. ③ Fix the smoke suite's two spurious passes and carry failing check SETS in the ledger, so future steering is not misled by totals.

## 2026-07-30 NIGHT (Opus 5, autonomous) — THE THROUGHPUT COLLAPSE IS THE HEADLINE, AND WE COULD NOT SEE IT

**Chasing why a run scored 15/23 led somewhere much bigger than the score.**

- **✅✅✅ T6-J SOLVED — `GraphWriter.Batch.commit()` IS ~78% OF THE LOOP'S WALL CLOCK, 93–95s EVERY CYCLE.** Five-phase timing inside `SmsCycleIngestor` accounts for the gap with **no remainder**: `sms-commit` **92,861 / 94,834 / 93,546 ms** across three cycles against measured gaps of 93.0s and 95.0s, while `batch-build` 128ms, `lineage-stamp` 14ms and `cycle-merge` 6ms are noise. One call in the `semantic-memory-substrate` **dependency**; everything this repo wrote around it is milliseconds.
  - **THE FIX IS A DESIGN CALL, NOT A TUNING ONE — left for the user.** ① take the ingest off the cycle's critical path (nothing in the cycle reads this memory back — where the measurements points, but it changes when memory becomes durable relative to a crash) ② make `commit()` cheaper, in a dependency this repo does not own ③ gate it and measure what the loop loses. **Worth roughly DOUBLING throughput** — 35 cycles per 90 min back toward the 62 the 23/23 run managed, which is larger than any mechanism built this week.
  - **THREE CONFIDENT WRONG ANSWERS FIRST, all recorded beside the right one:** *the observation gates* (measured 2–3s per firing, ~70 seconds per run, not 70 minutes) · *unindexed graph writes* (A/B with 15 indexes vs 3: **−21%**, real, minor, and kept) · *a one-time warm-up* (checked when a counter read `/1`; it was the readout lagging one cycle). Also eliminated by measurement: the T4-CJ `[*1..2]` lineage stamp (reaches **16 nodes**), `validateAllOrThrow` (no Cypher, in-memory), content-keyed Artifact merges (UUID ids, ≤123-char contents).
  - **What settled it was not better reasoning — it was splitting one suspect into five numbers** instead of reporting a total that would have restated the question.
- **🔴 T6-J AS ORIGINALLY FILED — cycle time has DOUBLED and 70 of 89 minutes of a run had no owner.** The user asked why a run capped and whether that was premature. Measuring it: the **23/23 run also ended on its cap**, at 81.9 min — and got **62 cycles**. Run `21-25-43` got **35** out of 88.6. Median cycle **77s → 153s**, p90 97 → 179, and **34 of 35 gaps exceed 120s against 2 of 62**. With a 60s scheduler floor, work per cycle went from ~17s to ~93s.
  - **What we measured explained almost nothing.** LLM + builds = 26.8 of 82 min on the good run, 18.9 of 88.6 on the recent one. `T5-P` timed builds and `T5-R` tracked tokens; **nothing timed the package gate, boot gate or criterion probes** — the operations that package an artifact and launch a JVM with a readiness wait of up to 60s.
  - **Prime suspect is this week's own observation machinery:** gate operations went **7 across 62 cycles → 23 across 35**, roughly 4× per cycle.
  - **BUILT (`4c51ddf`), measurement only:** `BuildCostTracker` widened to named categories (one home, `build` preserves T5-P's totals exactly), `package-gate`/`boot-gate` record themselves, per-cycle INFO readout, carried into `summary.txt` + ledger. **The self-check is deliberately NOT a category** — its elapsed time IS the two gates it calls, so a third bucket would sum to more than the run. 6 tests. **No gate frequency changed.**
  - **This reframes everything measured this week**, including T4-DA: its apparent 3-point cost sits inside a collapse that PREDATES it (frozen runs without it already at 125–140s/cycle).
- **✅ T4-DC BUILT (`ac3a651`) — and the build was not a detector, because the detector already existed.** `statusRiskFacts` already produced *"thrown exception type(s) [RuntimeException] are not covered by any mapped type"* on the 14/23 artifact and T3-BX had already wired it to the prompt. **The defect was TIMING:** the comparison ran only `if (anyThrow && anyStatusMapping)`, so it stayed silent until a handler existed — **cycle 24 of 25**. Both halves now reach evolution from the first throw. A naive fix would have misread the 23/23 artifact (no advice class, maps in controller catches) → catch clauses that yield a status now count as mappings. **Field: facts at cycle ~5 vs 24.** 7 tests.
- **🔴 T4-CU: ONE DEFECT, THREE MEASURED HARMS — closing a leaf on a model's READING of the source.** Run `21-25-43` closed *"Implement input validation for Book model"* COMPLETED on criteria that were false (`Book` had `@NotBlank/@Size/@Pattern`, `BookController` bound it with **no `@Valid`**, no manual validation anywhere). The harms: ① a false ✓ later gates inherit; ② **the probe DID catch it** (*"answered 201 to POST /books {}"*), raised a work item, decomposed it into that same false-COMPLETED child, and the parent was then retired `exhausted after 1 cycles without merge` → **GIVEN_UP — a correct repair abandoned because a leaf lied**; ③ **12 prompts assert both "validation COMPLETED" and "the constraints are not enforced"** — the loop contradicting itself in its own words, 42 renderings, `@Valid` never added.
  - **A cheap fix exists (no boot needed — the fact and the criterion contradict statically) and was DELIBERATELY NOT BUILT:** matching them needs "which endpoint does this controller method serve", i.e. more core coupling, on the day the user's direction became *"the core shouldn't be a patchwork of toycase code"*. Filed as **the first real exercise for the plug-in seam** ([[project-target-domain-plugin-design]]).
- **THE USER'S "is the toycase confusing it?" QUESTION, ANSWERED WITH MEASUREMENT: no — the domain facts are correct and the loop acts on them** (it built a five-handler `GlobalExceptionHandler` mapping every type it throws, the 23/23 architecture). The confusion is the loop's own bookkeeping asserting false completions beside true facts.
- **🔴 A RANKING I GOT WRONG, corrected by the user.** I called a 1119-char goal description carrying raw Tomcat log lines "smaller" than the contradiction. Measured: **279 tokens × 46 renders ≈ 12.9k tokens vs ~480** — **27×**, and it carries stale false defect claims too. Root cause found and fixed (`3cda615`): `BootGate.summariseOutput` fell back to the raw output tail whenever nothing looked diagnostic — which is exactly the behavioural-defect case. Now says *"the process logged nothing diagnostic"*. The T1-FJ path is pinned by a test.
- **T4-DA PUT BEHIND A SWITCH (`bcb83bf`, default ON)** — the only recent mechanism without one. Record: **OFF 18/18/18** (39–42 cycles, 16–20 merges) vs **ON 14, 15** (25/35 cycles, 10/13 merges). Two runs with confounds do not establish harm, and T6-J shows the collapse predates it — but it could not be settled without a switch. Its watch is at **2 of 11 control clauses** (baseline 0), so the benefit side is real and small.
- **T2-EX first native readings: 76 criteria authored, 58% executable.** Across four runs shares 71/31/74/58 against scores 18/18/18/15 — **the second-best share scored worst.** Measurements against the strong form of its premise; the ticket should narrow to observability.
- **A transient tool failure silently dropped a ticket edit** — checked rather than assumed, and re-applied. Also recorded [[feedback-never-edit-a-running-script]] after editing `preflight.sh` mid-run made four summary fields vanish with no error.
- **State:** suite **2359/0/5**, bookkeeping 0 warnings, census 435 (28 open). **~35 commits this session, ALL UNPUSHED.** Run `2026-07-30_23-22-06` in flight to measure gate cost — **no code changes during it.** Disk 91%.
- **NEXT, in order:** ① read the T6-J cost readout — if the gates own the missing 70 min, the options are fewer firings or one booted artifact reused across probes ② the plug-in seam design, with the T4-CU static-contradiction check as its first exercise ③ T4-DA A/B via the new switch, but only AFTER T6-J, since the throughput handicap confounds it ④ nothing about gate frequency ships before its cost is known.

### ⚠ SESSION CLOSE — READ THESE FOUR FIRST

1. **`GraphWriter.Batch.commit()` costs ~94s EVERY CYCLE (8 samples: 92.9 · 94.8 · 93.5 · 93.5 · 121.3 · 97.3 · 94.2 · 92.1).** ~78% of the loop's wall clock, in a DEPENDENCY. Fixing it roughly doubles throughput and outweighs every mechanism built this week. **Three options are on `T6-J`; the choice is a design call about the memory substrate, deliberately left unmade.**
2. **Untested cross-run hypothesis, condition written down:** whether graph size drives that cost. 77s/cycle on 2026-07-28 vs 153s on 07-30 makes it plausible; the probe run moved the graph only 65,360→65,987 and does NOT test it. Needs one build against graphs of very different sizes.
3. **`T4-CO` carries a proposed `[s]` SUBSUMED awaiting the user** — its scope was twice set by their decision, so the call is theirs.
4. **Config defaults changed tonight:** `autobuilder.analysis.seed-coverage.enabled=true` (T4-DA, new switch — OFF is the A/B arm). Ingest-path indexes now created at boot (`Neo4jDeferredMutationGraphService.ensureSchema`), worth a measured 21% and previously existing only in one local database.

## 2026-07-30 LATE (Opus 5, user-directed → autonomous) — THE 18→23 GAP IS MEASURED AND IT IS NOT WHAT THE ROADMAP SAID

**The user asked why we still don't get 23/23, and why nothing checks that the subgoals collectively still solve the seed. Both questions turned out to have measurable answers, and neither matched the standing plan.**

- **🔴 THE 18→23 GAP, MEASURED BY WHERE ATTENTION WENT — `T4-DB` FILED, and it displaces `T4-CO` as the top item.** Classifying every worked subgoal across the three frozen runs (122 cycles) by the status it names: **400 → 38 cycles · 404 → 13 · 500 → 6 · 409 → 5 · 422 → ZERO.** The checks failing in all three runs are `[14]`/`[15]` (404) and `[19]` (422). Lining those up against `DeterministicProbeSynthesizer` leaves **no residue**: `absentResource` abstains unless the PATH is parameterized, so `POST /loans` with an unknown `bookId` in the BODY was unprobeable; the bounded-count rule abstains by design. **Every check it CAN build a probe for passes.** The loop repairs what it can observe and fails exactly what it cannot — and **31% of its cycles went to a `POST /loans` 400 requirement that no smoke check measures**. Attention follows the probes.
- **`T4-DB` slice 1 SHIPPED (`2704684`) + replay-validated.** A reference carried in the body is now probed: the body comes from the artifact's own model and every field the CRITERION NAMES is pointed at an unused id (whole-word match, so "id" does not claim `bookId`; a criterion naming no field yields no probe; textual fields keep their shape). **Replay over the three frozen corpora: 5 and 19 new probes where there were none, and the distinct shapes are exactly `POST /loans {"bookId":999999999…}` and `{…"memberId":999999999}` — smoke `[14]` and `[15]`.** Pinned against the criterion text a real run wrote.
- **A parser fix came out of it (`3261e77`):** the status pattern demanded the literal word `HTTP`, so *"POST /loans returns 404 Not Found …"* — verbatim from a real tree, and smoke `[14]` itself — never parsed. A response verb now marks a status, bounded 100–599. It reverses a deliberate earlier decision, so the test carries the measurements and pins that "at most 120 chars" is still refused.
- **🔴 THEN THE MEASUREMENT CORRECTED ME, and this is the biggest lever found all session — `T2-EX` FILED.** I called parser recall the bottleneck. **It is not.** Per-run, parse rate tracks the rate at which criteria NAME AN ENDPOINT almost exactly — **77%→71% · 32%→31% · 79%→74%** — so the parser converts ~93% of what is addressed to an endpoint. Run `06-28-19` is dark because only **32%** of its criteria name one at all: it wrote STRUCTURAL criteria where the others wrote behavioural ones. **How much of a run is verifiable is decided at criterion-authoring time and swings more than two to one between runs of the same build and seed.** Slice 1 yields 5 and 19 probes in the behavioural runs and **zero** in that one, from identical code. Not owned by `T2-ED` (which grades structural criteria at VERIFICATION time — helps them pass, does nothing for probing). It also explains the 07-28 `T4-CU` reach collapse without needing plan granularity.
- **`T4-DA` BUILT AND FIELD-VERIFIED — the user's second question, and the framing is theirs: THE POINT IS COMMUNICATION, NOT GATING.** `SeedCoverageAnalyzer` compares the seed's own clauses against every subgoal in any status plus every criterion, reporting what no work item articulates and what no criterion would prove; `SeedCoverageBlock` renders it into the planner, evolution and analysis prompts. Deterministic, no model call, no domain vocabulary. Injection through `injectCoverageGaps` deliberately UNWIRED. **Honest scoping recorded on the ticket: this is NOT the 18→23 gap** — in the frozen runs the failing requirements were already articulated, probed and contradicted. It is insurance against false completion #2's class.
- **🔴🔴 T4-DA'S TWO FIELD DEFECTS ARE THE METHODOLOGY LESSON OF THE SESSION. BOTH SURVIVED A GREEN 2325-TEST SUITE AND AN OFFLINE REPLAY OVER THREE REAL CORPORA; BOTH SURFACED WITHIN MINUTES OF A LIVE RUN.**
  - **First firing:** `0 unclaimed` against a tree holding only the root — `getAllGoals()` returns the root and **the root's description IS the seed**, so everything was covered by construction. It would have answered "no drift" forever while looking healthy. The offline prototype had excluded the root by array index; production did not.
  - **Second firing, and it made the run STERILE:** `SeedCoverageReadout.isEmpty()` serializes as a property `empty` the canonical constructor cannot read, so `meta.json` failed to load and `MutationSnapshotService` **rewrote it with `mutationState=REJECTED`**. Run `17-37-09`: **9 mutations ACCEPTED, 0 merged, 38 persist failures.** Any run carrying it could not merge anything.
  - **The convention already existed and I did not follow it** — all four sibling records on `CycleContextData` carry `@JsonIgnore` on exactly such an accessor, and `T1-EV` is a closed ticket for the same class. **What was missing was enforcement**, so `CycleContextDataRoundTripTest` now walks the context's record components by reflection and fails on any accessor Jackson writes that the constructor cannot read — keyed on the TYPE, so a record added later is covered without editing a test. Verified non-vacuous by removing the annotation and watching it name the offender.
  - Folded into [[feedback-short-field-run-after-context-change]]: replay decides whether a change HELPS; only the field shows whether it is WIRED.
- **Also recorded:** [[feedback-no-symbolic-enumerations]] (no ①②③ anywhere — name the items; positional tokens carry no meaning on re-read).
- **✅✅ T4-DB SLICE 1 VALIDATED END-TO-END AGAINST THE REAL FAILING ARTIFACT — replay proves the probe is BUILT, this proves it WORKS.** Booted run `08-00-00`'s preserved 18/23 artifact and sent the exact request the synthesizer emits: `POST /loans {"bookId":999999999,…}` → **500**, body `{"message":"Book not found"}`, where the spec requires **404**. Byte-identical to what smoke `[14]`/`[15]` report on that artifact. **The probe detects precisely the defect failing in every frozen run, for which no probe existed at all before this slice.** It also confirms the frozen-series reading at the finest grain: the application KNOWS the book is missing and says so in the body — only the exception→status mapping is wrong.
- **🔴 AND THEN THE LINK AFTER IT — `T2-ET` EXTENDED, measured across all three frozen runs.** Self-check findings DO become work items, and then: `SUBSUMED (reason=REPLACED, resolution='superseded by replanning')` — **3 · 2 · 2 measurements-backed items discarded per run**, every one raised from observed behaviour of the running artifact. **This is the mechanism behind "the loop was told and shipped it anyway."** Stated conservatively because it is not universally harmful: run `06-28-19`'s superseded item carries a COMPLETED child (replanning decomposed it), while run `04-56-38`'s two `POST /loans` items name defects the final artifact still exhibits. **Recorded on T2-ET, not filed separately** — the invariant is *a work item raised by observed measurements may be closed only by measurement showing that the observation no longer holds*; T2-ET names the score half, this is the replan half, and covering one is the two-patches shape. First move stays measurement (stamp `originatingGate` survivability through the replan path).
- **🔴 A COST CLAIM OF MINE, MADE AND THEN RETRACTED THE SAME HOUR.** I recorded T4-DA's readout as plausibly costing +16–25% cycle time. **The token accounting refutes it:** the block is **4658 of 200109 prompt tokens (2.3%)** while **evaluation alone is 72%** (median evaluation prompt ~16k tokens). A 2.3% increase cannot produce a 20% slowdown, and the run then demonstrated the real cause by stalling in `LLMClient` timeout retries with the model alive at 85% GPU. **What survives: the block is 24% of the EVOLUTION prompt, a real dilution risk for the agent that writes code. What does not: that it costs a run eight cycles.** Cost side now looks small; benefit side still n=1. The correction is on the ticket AND in the watch, so the three-run evaluation cannot inherit the wrong framing.
- **INSTRUMENTS COMMITTED, not left in a scratchpad** ([[project-log-and-backup-conventions]] / the T6-E ephemerality lesson): `scripts/clause_coverage.py` + `clause_control_df2a907.txt` (10 tests) — the control is the **11 of 16 seed clauses no run of the frozen series ever articulates**, baseline 0 per run, all three controls verified at 0. `preflight.sh` now writes `clauses_closed_vs_control`, `median_cycle_seconds`, `criteria_executable`, `criteria_authored`; `outcome_ledger.py` carries all four (17 tests). **The three-run question now accumulates by itself.**
- **State:** suite **2345/0/5**, bookkeeping 0 warnings, census 433 (26 open). **~20 commits this session, ALL UNPUSHED.** Live run `2026-07-30_18-39-00` in flight — 13 cycles, 7 merges, 0 persist failures, 1 of 11 control clauses closed; it stalled ~12 min in LLM retries mid-run, so **its cycle-time data is contaminated and it should not count as one of the three watch runs unless re-run**. Disk 91%.
- **NEXT, in order:** ① finish the live run and check whether the planner/evolution acts on a clause the readout named (T4-DA's remaining DoD half) ② `T2-EX` measurement first — a per-run executable-criteria readout, NO steering change before six runs of data ③ `T4-DB` slice 2 (bounded-count) is blocked upstream: the 422 criterion a real run wrote does not parse at all, naming neither verb nor path ④ `T4-CO` should be re-read against T4-DB before anyone builds it — same family, wrong level.

## 2026-07-30 (Opus 5, autonomous) — T4-CY COST REMOVED AND RE-ENABLED · T6-G GATE CLOSES THE RECONCILIATION CHAIN · TWO TICKETS SHIPPED WHOSE DEFECT WAS WIRING, NOT LOGIC

**Session shape: five commits, four tickets moved, and three of them turned out to be about something other than what they said.**

- **T4-CY RE-ENABLED (`8ceaf73`).** `DeterministicProbeSynthesizer` builds probe sequences from the artifact's own sources with **zero model calls** — `400` → asserted request with an empty body · `404` → an id nothing created · `409` → a derived body sent twice. Measured over the live seed spec: **8 of 11 stated requirements executable**, where all eleven previously cost a synthesis call that could block for minutes. Model path survives as `synthesizeWithModel` behind a default-OFF flag.
  - **🔴 THE FIRST CUT WAS 9 of 11 AND TWO OF THE NINE WERE FALSE-CONTRADICTION GENERATORS.** Reading the probes instead of counting them: **`422` de-scoped** (its only instance here is "at most 3 active loans" — an empty body is refused as invalid input long before any limit, and reporting that 400 as a missing 422 is the harm class that got T2-EU and T2-EV reverted) · **a refused SETUP request is now an abstain** (the duplicate-loan probe's first POST is refused when the book/member it references do not exist; without the guard the second POST's 404 reads as "409 expected, got 404") · **the server-assigned `id` is dropped from creation bodies**. A test also caught a real defect first: `ground()` used `String.replaceFirst`, so `$1.id` was read as a regex group reference and threw.
- **T4-CY OBSERVABILITY, and it paid for itself in ten minutes (`4b810d5`).** Every reason for not observing was silent or at `debug`, so "never looked" was indistinguishable from "looked and found nothing" — the same blindness that let `criterion_probe_runs=0` stand for 14 runs. Now each abstention logs its reason at INFO. **The very first field run then said `the last compile probe was COMPILE_ERROR`, which is a diagnosis I could not get at all before.** Also fixed: the interval reset BEFORE the cheap gates, so a free skip burned a whole interval (run `21-39-59` got 4 looks in 41 cycles, was mid-repair at every one, and observed nothing); it now resets only where a package build and boot are actually spent.
- **T6-G MEASURED — RESULT THIN, and the reconciliation chain closes on measurements (`3015196`).** `scripts/lineage_overlap.py` + 16 tests. The fixture was **26 lineages, not the 2 the ticket assumed**. 91.2% of criteria and 93.4% of goal descriptions are unique to ONE lineage; `codeDigest` — the identity axis the chain was to be built on — is populated on **2.4%** of criterion results; ecosystem facts are not per-lineage at all (one shared on-disk cache); **59 of the 87 shared criteria (67.8%) carry OPPOSITE results**.
  - **The number that decided it came from refusing the aggregate.** The script's first result was MARGINAL at 6.6%; reading the 111 "transferable" items showed every one was the seed's root goal, its opening decomposition, or a template repair. Splitting overlap by the role that PRODUCED each goal turns that reading into a measurement: `system` 100% · `coverage-retro` 18.6% · `planner` 10.0% · **`analysis` — the loop's own discoveries — 3 of 288 = 1.0%** · `divided-criterion`/`verification-residue` 0%. On the original two-lineage fixture: **0 of 25**. Recorded on T4-CN/T4-CM/T4-CS with re-entry = a DIFFERENT-seed fixture or a semantic pass. **T6-H keeps independent value and is NOT closed.**
- **T3-BX (`d43f286`) — the rule already existed; its output never reached the agent that could act on it.** `WiringFactsAnalyzer`'s validation-surface check has been emitting exactly this fact for months. Over the real output trees of **six** corpora it fires on **all six** (incl. *"requests validation of Loan but Loan declares no field constraint annotations"* — why `POST /loans` answered 500 where the spec asks 400), while `peerAnnotationAnomalies()` — **the ONLY analyzer path into the evolution prompt** — returns **0 on all six**. `analyze()` goes to the criteria verifier and nowhere else. Shipped `statusRiskFacts()` (a status-relevant SUBSET, not the whole payload) into the evolution prompt, plus peer naming in the existing single home. **I first built a second vocabulary-free detector (132 lines), it worked, and I deleted it — one invariant, one home.**
- **✅ T2-CI CLOSED ON A FIELD FIRING, first run it was live (`2026-07-30_00-48-16`, 01:13:29) — result RIGHT, reasoning 3/5 NOISE, precision fixed the same hour.** The subgoal was "Create BookService.java", so five annotation losses across three files was suspicious enough to CHECK rather than celebrate. **True positive:** `BookRepository` lost `@Repository` with member names unchanged while `BookService` still constructor-injected it — unregistered bean, context fails to start, **file compiles perfectly**, i.e. exactly what the 2026-05-22 subsumption-by-compile-probe closure claimed was impossible. My heuristic was the ONLY dissenting voice (LLM said ACCEPTED 0.85; `DeterministicScoreHeuristic` said ACCEPTED) and its CRITICAL defect finding carried the rollback. **False component:** the same mutation dropped `@Data`/`@NoArgsConstructor`/`@AllArgsConstructor` from `Book` **and hand-wrote the eight members they generated** — a real substitution, wrongly flagged, with rationale telling evolution to keep the annotation. **Fix:** substitution is now recognised at the MEMBER level (a lost annotation is excused when the type declares member names it did not before; compared by NAME so a changed signature cannot launder a loss). Replaying the real snapshot against the real mutation drops the report **5 losses → 1**, and a faithful `@Service`-with-unchanged-members reconstruction still REJECTS. **Recall traded away and pinned by its own test:** add-a-member-plus-drop-a-stereotype is no longer reported (bounded by the boot gate and the peer rule). It broke one of my own earlier tests whose fixture happened to add a method while stripping the annotation. **Diagnosed against the per-cycle SNAPSHOT — the live tree changed between two of my own commands, so [[feedback-verify-at-decision-timestamp]] caught in the act.**
- **T2-CI as built (`14235c4`) needed none of the store, the marker list, or the goal-text override the ticket proposed.** `previousOutput()` already IS the record of what earlier merges established. `MergeRegressionHeuristic` rejects a candidate whose type loses annotations and adds none (strict loss), which also dissolves the migration override: a package migration rewrites imports and leaves simple names alone. **Verified on real artifacts** — stripping the stereotype from four corpora's actual controllers reproduces the field defect REJECTED 4/4, unchanged controls abstain 4/4; both sides of every pair compile, which is why the 2026-05-22 subsumption-by-compile-probe closure was wrong.
- **🔧 METHODOLOGY UNLOCK, measured: tests and live runs are NOT mutually exclusive.** `logback-spring.xml` takes its destination from `FILE_BASE_PATH` alone, so `FILE_BASE_PATH=<scratch> mvn test` runs the **full 2279-test suite during a live run with the live log byte-identical** (429257 → 429257, then growing normally). This removed the serialisation that used to make offline work wait out a 90-minute run. Folded into [[project-tests-rotate-live-logs]] as its third variant.
- **HARNESS FIX (`46923d1`):** preflight's G1 guard scanned every `/proc` cmdline for the jar name without checking the process was a JVM, so my own run monitor — which grepped for that name — was counted as five live JVMs and blocked a relaunch. Third time this self-match family has bitten. Guard now requires `comm == java`; `scripts/autobuilder_up.sh` holds the pattern so no caller's command line carries it.
- **MY RECURRING ERROR APPEARED TWICE MORE, both caught:** ① I read a `NOT_EVALUATED` on four real artifacts as the new heuristic failing on real input — the **probe** was passing a null `EvaluationOutcome`, which every heuristic here treats as no-opinion. The code was right; the instrument was not. ② I spent several steps hunting a phantom interval defect from a cycle count of 9 that was actually **5** (my `awk /CYCLE /` matched other lines; `behaviour.ndjson` `cycle_start` is the honest count).
- **✅✅ T4-CY PROVEN IN THE FIELD (run `2026-07-30_02-23-01`, 02:46:45): `durationMs=4674, requirements=11, finding=yes`.** **4.7 SECONDS** vs the 29.5 / 14.9 MINUTES that got it disabled — ~200x, same 11 requirements. It injected *"the application exits at startup (exit 1)"* carrying the artifact's own `UnsatisfiedDependencyException … No qualifying bean of type '…BookRepository'`; ground truth confirmed `BookRepository` had no `@Repository`, and **the loop targeted it correctly** (repair mutation writes `@Repository`). I first suspected mis-targeting from the model's prose and the diff refuted it.
  - **🔴 The repair never merged — MY OWN member-loss rule rejected it** (that mutation also dropped two `Book` constructors). The run's interpretation agent named the heuristic as the cause of a stagnation pattern. **Member-loss half now OFF by default** (`autobuilder.heuristic.merge-regression.member-losses-enabled:false`); annotation half stays ON (5 field firings, all real registration losses, artifact kept every annotation they defended). Field scorecard that decided it: the member half caught **nothing the annotation half would have missed** and cost a run. Re-enable condition recorded: a field truncation whose annotations stay intact.
  - **T2-CI's member half also taught the gain test twice:** a service and then a controller each moved `@Autowired` from constructor to field — no member gained, type annotations untouched — so the gain test now also counts annotations added to MEMBERS.
- **RUN `2026-07-30_02-58-04` (deadline, 43 cycles, 11 merges, 7 files, `artifact-build-failed`) — CLEAN of my regressions and it exposed the real blocker.** T2-CI **0 firings** (member arm gated), so no stagnation. T4-CY **0 observations** — 21 abstains, 14 `COMPILE_ERROR` + 7 `not packageable`. Cause: `Unable to find main class`, **zero entry points**, again. **That is 7 of 7 `artifact-build-failed` runs on the same message, five consecutively.**
- **✅ T4-CZ BUILT (narrow) — and I REVISED my own "this needs the user's call" position, on grounds worth recording.** Two new facts changed it: ① the measurements reached 7/7 with five consecutive, ② **the frozen-build series is USELESS until this is fixed** — you cannot measure variance in a metric deterministically clamped at the floor, and `artifact-build-failed` is a floor. So this became the precondition for the measurement I had just argued should come first.
  - **The scope never mentions packaging, which is what makes it categorically unlike the reverted T2-EQ check.** `requireEntryPointNow` asks only whether an entry point EXISTS via `EntryPointScan.hasJavaEntryPointInSources` — a pure in-memory scan of sources already on the cycle context. No package build, no boot, nothing that can be mid-repair. T2-EQ demanded a whole-project property (packageability) of a partial skeleton on every merge; this asserts a scaffold requirement satisfiable in one file. The requirement itself is **T4-CV's, unchanged** (same item text, criterion and `injectCoverageGaps` path, so T1-DE dedup + T1-FG idempotency are free) — **only the timing changes.** Behind `autobuilder.goal.entry-point-required-early` (default true, one switch to reverse).
  - Verified against the real corpora before shipping: the 4 failed runs (7/1/5/2 files, no entry point) all "would require it"; the 22-file run that scored stays silent. Suite **2303/0/5**.
- **🔴 THE FROZEN SERIES' FIRST RUN FOUND A DEFECT OF MINE IN 14 MINUTES — twin work items from a volatile finding text.** T4-CY observed the same two unreachable endpoints at 04:43 and 04:50 and injected **TWO ACTIVE TWINS** of one goal. The findings were **1286 characters each, differing in about forty** — ISO timestamp, pid, servlet thread name, ephemeral port, relayed from the boot log straight into the work-item description. That defeated **three layers at once**, all of which key on that text: my suppression guard (which I had documented as working), T1-DE paraphrase filtering, and T1-FG per-gap idempotency. A defect the loop had not yet fixed re-minted itself every interval and **inflated the backlog — the one property that separates every good run from every bad one**. Fixed in `BootGate.summariseOutput` (strip per-run tokens, keep the substance; verified identical on the two real findings) + regression test. `df2a907`, suite **2304/0/5**.
  - **I broke the code freeze twice, both times because the instrument was broken rather than because I wanted to build** — first for T4-CZ (a metric clamped at the floor measures nothing), then for this (a run that mints twins measures a bug). Both are legitimate; both are worth watching as a pattern, because "the instrument is broken" is an easy story to tell oneself.
  - **THE POSITIVE from that run, before it was stopped:** T4-CZ fired at **3 minutes** (vs never), the loop wrote `LibraryApplication.java` within **3 cycles**, and 100 seconds later **T4-CY could observe for the first time in that run** — the dependency made visible: `entry point → packageable → observable`. Probes executed: `probed=6 skipped=5 contradicted=3`, three cost samples **4674 / 4938 / 4751 ms** against the 29.5 and 14.9 MINUTES that got the mechanism disabled.
## ⚠ SESSION CLOSE 2026-07-30 — READ THESE FIVE THINGS FIRST

1. **RESOLVED before session end — no action needed.** An uncommitted `CycleExecutor.java` edit (not mine) had gated the self-check on the entry-point requirement: `if (behaviouralSelfCheck != null && entryPointRequired)`. That inverts the intent — the check would run ONLY when an entry point was just found missing, which is exactly when the artifact cannot be packaged or observed, so it would produce no observations at all. **The user rolled it back; verified clean** (tree matches HEAD, zero `entryPointRequired` references, the self-check runs unconditionally as the frozen series validated, suite 2304/0/5 BUILD SUCCESS). The frozen series was never affected — the edit postdated run 3 by ~90 minutes, and run 1 performed 6 self-checks where that gate permits at most 2.
   - **Worth knowing if the idea returns:** the two mechanisms are sequential, not conditional. `requireEntryPointNow` makes the artifact *packageable*; the self-check then *observes* it. Gating the second on the first inverts the dependency the series demonstrated — T4-CZ fired at 3 min, the entry point landed within 3 cycles, and T4-CY could observe 100 seconds later precisely BECAUSE the requirement had already been satisfied.
2. **`T4-CO` (status-code binding) is now the top open item, on measured measurements** — the frozen series shows the whole 18→23 gap is status mapping. It is currently `[>]` deferred. See its re-entry block.
3. **KNOWN DEFECT left unfixed on purpose:** T4-CZ's stale-view re-raise (reads the cycle-start file map, so it re-raises one cycle after the loop satisfies it). Filed with its timeline and its fix on the ticket; watch `t4cz-stale-view-reraise`.
4. **Config defaults CHANGED tonight** — a future session should know without reading five tickets: `autobuilder.build.self-check.enabled=true` (T4-CY back ON) · `autobuilder.build.criterion-probe.llm-fallback-enabled=false` (model synthesis off; deterministic path is primary) · `autobuilder.heuristic.merge-regression.member-losses-enabled=false` (member arm off on measured cost; annotation + coverage arms ON) · `autobuilder.goal.entry-point-required-early=true` (T4-CZ).
5. **26 commits on main, ALL UNPUSHED — I never push** ([[feedback-no-auto-commit]]). Suite **2304/0/5**, bookkeeping 0 warnings, no JVM, no driver shells, watch registry 10→13. Disk **91%** (82G free) — five 90-minute runs tonight; `/tmp/preflight_runs` is the growth.

## ✅ FROZEN-BUILD SERIES COMPLETE (`df2a907`, 3 × 90 min, ONE build) — THE CEILING IS STATUS-CODE MAPPING, AND THE LOOP ALREADY SEES IT

| run | terminal | smoke | cycles | merges | files | entry pt | self-checks | T2-CI |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 04-56-38 | deadline | **18/23** | 42 | 16 | 11 | 1 | 6 | 2 |
| 06-28-19 | deadline | **18/23** | 39 | 20 | 13 | 1 | 8 | 2 |
| 08-00-00 | deadline | **18/23** | 39 | 20 | 20 | 1 | 7 | 0 |

- **🔴 ALL 15 FAILURES ACROSS THE THREE RUNS ARE WRONG STATUS CODES. NOT ONE IS MISSING FUNCTIONALITY.** Replayed the suite against each preserved artifact to get the per-check breakdown. The response bodies prove the domain logic is right — `"Book not found"`, `"Member has reached maximum number of active loans"`, `"Book is already on loan"` — only the exception→status mapping is wrong. **So the whole gap between 18/23 and 23/23 is one diagnosable family: `T4-CO` status-code binding, currently DEFERRED. It should be the top open item.**
- **What is stable is the COUNT, not the SET — so the identical 18/23 is partly coincidence.** `[14]`, `[15]`, `[19]` fail in all three; the other two move between `[16]`, `[20]`, `[21]`, `[22]`. And the SAME semantic defect returns a different wrong status each run: "not found" came back **409 → 422 → 500** on `[14]`/`[15]`. The loop is reliably wrong about mapping and unreliably wrong about which code.
- **🔴 THE LOOP WAS TOLD AND SHIPPED IT ANYWAY.** T4-CY's probes reported these mismatches DURING the runs (`POST /loans → 400, but answered 500`; `POST /books → 400, answered 201`) and T3-BX rendered the static cause into the evolution prompt **six times** in run 1. This is no longer an observation problem: the information arrives and the repair does not stick. **That is the gap to design against — and it is more support for the session's structural finding that this codebase's constraint is connecting/acting on facts it already computes, not acquiring new ones.**
- **Files grew 11 → 13 → 20 with the score flat at 18** — more code did not help, which independently supports "the residual is not missing implementation".
- **Variance is LOWER than the project believed, once the floor is gone.** The standing belief was "same build, 23/23 then 11/23 — single runs cannot adjudicate". That spread was dominated by the build-failure floor (unscorable vs scored). With T4-CZ removing it, three runs landed on the same number, so **a small number of runs CAN now adjudicate a mechanism change** — the frozen series' own justification, established by running it.
- **Reproducibility of the new mechanisms across all three:** entry point present **1/1/1** (T4-CZ never missed), self-checks **6/8/7** at 4.3–5.6 s each (~0.7% of wall clock), T2-CI **2/2/0**, zero twins.

## 2026-07-29 (Opus 5, continuation — ~36h session) — SELF-CORRECTION PROVEN THEN DISABLED ON COST; "REGRESSION" WAS A MISFRAMING

**The loop observed its own running behaviour for the first time, and I had to switch it off.**
- **T4-CY WORKED (run `2026-07-28_23-16-15`).** Two mid-run observations; the criterion probes **executed for the first time in 14 runs** (`probed=6 contradicted=5`, then `6/3`); the defects were REAL (`POST /books`→500 vs spec 400, `GET /members/{}`→500 vs 404) and became work items. **The loop CAN see its own behaviour while it still has budget to fix it.**
- **🔴 DISABLED ON COST (`bc7aef1`), and my cost model was wrong TWICE.** Observations cost **29.5 + 14.9 min** at 73/77 requirements, then **19.7 + 14.9 min** at only 11. Neither list-length fix touched the cause: the time is in probe-**SYNTHESIS** calls that **TIME OUT** — `LLMClient` escalates its read timeout across retries and the check makes model calls **synchronously inside the cycle, competing with the loop's own use of the same local model**. Throughput 0.16–0.29 cyc/min vs ~0.65 normal; circuit breaker tripped twice. **RE-ENABLE only with DETERMINISTIC synthesis** — `RequestBodySynthesizer` + `{}` + an unused id + a repeated body cover the common cases in milliseconds.
- **T2-EW CORRECTED — I guarded one door of three.** Run `2026-07-29_00-54-22` failed on broken `BookControllerTest`/`BookServiceTest` while the log shows `MergeAgent` had **SKIPPED exactly those files**. `MutationApplier` writes evolution's mutations straight into `output/`; `CompileProbeService` stages there too. Now one home: `UnrequestedTestSourcePolicy`, consulted by both writers.
- **🔴 THE "REGRESSION" WAS A MISFRAMING OF MINE.** User asked why Jul 28→29 runs regressed. Answer: **they didn't, systematically.** July 28 in order: `build-failed, build-failed, build-failed, 23/23, 11/23, 10/23, 20/23, 20/23` — the day OPENED with three build failures. **23/23 was a PEAK, not a plateau.** Session scored runs: `21,19,21,22,23,11,10,20,20,14` → **mean 18.1, best 23, ≥22 in 2/10**. I called the 23/23 "the third 23/23" and treated it as a baseline; it was one sample, and I measured everything after against a high-water mark.
- **PROVEN NOT the cause** (each checked, not assumed): the 11/23 and 10/23 predate T4-CY and the only production change between them and the 23/23 (`c374758`) sits in a `GATE_BOOT` branch that logged **0 executions** in both. Model is healthy (22 GB on GPU, realistic 8 KB prompt → **3.46 s**, deterministic at temp 0 — already 0, hardcoded at `LLMClient:92`). Prompt size per call **flat** all session. Graph growth irrelevant (558 `Goal` nodes of 86k).
- **THE ONE DISCRIMINATOR THAT HOLDS: ~40% of runs never empty their backlog**, and that separates every good run from every bad one. Reaching the package gate is **necessary but not sufficient** (5 of 10 that reached it still failed). No process metric predicts a good run at n=15 — plan inflation (12 vs 23 injections) overlaps, stagnation rate shows no separation.
- **MY RECURRING ERROR, four times today:** verified a mechanism **FIRED** and inferred the **outcome followed** (dedup collapse, DI-check suspicion, T2-EW trap, T2-EW multi-door). [[feedback-activity-not-progress]] already names this; checking the artifact costs seconds.

Live ceiling now **90 min**. Disk **91% full**.

## 2026-07-27 (Opus 5, autonomous → user-corrected) — T2-EQ FIELD-VERIFIED IN 3 RUNS: SHIPPED-YESTERDAY DESIGN WAS WRONG, USER RE-AIMED IT, HARM GONE
**The session was "begin the next verification run" and the run falsified the thing it was verifying. Twice. Then the user reframed the problem and the third run came back healthy.**
- **RUN 1 (`2026-07-27_13-01-43`) — T2-EQ's per-merge packaging check DETECTED correctly and made the run WORSE.** Predictions were written into the roadmap before the run ([[project-coverage-guard-experiment]]), which is what made a partial pass legible as a failure. Gate fired **52 s in** (baseline: 0 invocations in 65 cycles), relayed the tool's words, cost ~2 s — and the run stalled at **8.2 min with ONE Java file**. Two defects, both mine: ① the repair item named the file that happened to merge, so its first criterion was **already true** → evolution answered `#NoDiff` **16 times in 27 cycles** (same shape T4-AU's slice fixed for MISSING_POM); ② `MergeAgent`'s COMPILE_OK branch ran T1-DR's clear FIRST, so the item was **destroyed and re-created every merge** and the subsumed copies were rendered to the model as "already resolved" while the failure was live.
- **REPAIR SHIPPED, then RUN 2 EXPOSED THE REAL ERROR.** With the item fixed the model complied — and wrote **two** entry points, causing a fresh failure ("cannot find a single main class"). At cycle 11: 5 files, **2 of them entry points**, gate results `ERROR ERROR ERROR OK OK ERROR ERROR`. **USER CALL (the correction that mattered): "The first steps are not the 'whole' build of the project. We should be happy if the individual classes compile. The full package should be compilable in the end stage."** Per-merge verification demands a whole-project property of a half-built one. They chose enforcement **at the halt edge only**.
- **`PreHaltPackagingCheck` (new, `core/build`)** — called from the two halt edges that actually fire (`RootConvergenceTracker` convergence stall, `AutobuilderService` stagnation runaway). On failure it **injects the repair and the run keeps going instead of dying** — the same measurements class the stall edge already counts as progress. Self-bounding: nothing is created while a build-fix item is open, so one rescue per unresolved failure and an honest stall still halts. What stays true from the ticket: the requirement was **invisible, not merely late** — `PackageGate` hangs off closure and `autoCompleteRootIfSatisfied` early-returns while any subgoal is ACTIVE, which is exactly a stalling run's state.
- **RUN 3 (`2026-07-27_13-29-30`, deadline, 57 cycles / 33 merges / 18 files) — HARM GONE.** 0 merge-path invocations; gate ran only at closure edges and **passed every time**; the loop reached a **booting, serving application** (BootGate green) with root closure blocked on a *behavioural* defect (`POST /loans` → 500). First run of the day to get there.
- **🔴 HONEST RESIDUAL — the rescue path has NEVER FIRED IN THE FIELD.** `t2eq-halt-edge-rescue` = NO-OPPORTUNITY (run 3 ended on the time cap, not a halt). Unit coverage only: 5 tests on the check + 2 on the stall edge deferring to it. **Needs a run that actually halts with an unpackageable tree.** `t2eq-packaging-item-retired` also unfired. Both registered in `scripts/watches.json` (registry 6→8).
- **Run 3's `artifact-build-failed` is a DIFFERENT defect — do not attribute it to T2-EQ.** Final tree has `javax.validation` imports against a jakarta-era config; **verified it reproduces on an incremental compile**, so it is not a clean-vs-warm scoring artifact. Regressed in a 12-cycle tail after peaking at 45/57, still mid-repair at the cap, with its classifier steering the wrong way (proposing to ADD the javax API rather than fix the imports). **This is `T2-CI`'s harm class with a second independent sample** — T2-CI was reopened earlier the same day (~01:00) and this strengthens it.
- **T2-ET FILED (T2, open, NOT built):** a work item raised by a deterministic gate is closed by an LLM score — observed twice in run 2 (conf 0.86 / 0.78) while the package gate was failing that same cycle. T4-CC gave the gate authority over accept/reject and stamps `originatingGate` for it; `CycleExecutor`'s closure path has no such deference. Harm measured here was LOW (the gate re-raised it) — filed rather than built per gate-before-building. **Liveness note on the same ticket:** `t4cc-decisive-accept` = OPPORTUNITY-NO-FIRE(**13**) in run 3, so T4-CC's built half may itself be inert.
- **✅ RUN 4 (`2026-07-27_14-42-19`, 180-min ceiling — the 45-min cap was the user's call and it mattered): `GOAL_FULFILLED`, `SMOKE: 21/23` final AND preserved, 77 cycles / 19 merges / 62 min, drift tail 0, peaked at the final cycle.** The loop produced a 21/23 artifact **by itself** — the earlier 21/23 was measured by hand-adding an entry point to a copy of `output/`. Best recorded run since the 23/23 of 2026-07-07. Verified independently: `mvn clean package -DskipTests` OK, jar boots, `GET /books` 200, 21 of the 23 behaviour checks pass.
  - **The two failures are both status codes, and one confirms `T3-BX` from a different run and a different resource:** `[10] POST /members missing name` → **201, spec wants 400** (`Member` carries no constraint while `Book` does — so the sharper form is *constraints present on one type, absent on its peer*, exactly the odd-one-out shape `T3-BW` already computes; its build gate is now met) · `[22] POST return absent` → **409, spec wants 404** (not-found routed to the rule-violation handler).

## 2026-07-26 evening (Opus 5, user-directed) — GRAPH LINEAGE SEPARATION FINISHED: T6-F + T4-CJ slice 2 + T1-D shipped and FIELD-VERIFIED
**User challenge that started it: "the runs all connect to one big graph instead of letting the previous ones being historical."** Their proposed cause (roots merged by matching description string) was checked and is NOT what happens — goals are `MERGE {id: goal-<uuid>}` and nothing keys on text. The real coupling was ① lineage RESUME (GoalStack carries the root id, so runs of one lineage legitimately share a tree) and ② reads that ignored the run/lineage stamps. Their proposed INTERVENTION POINT was sound even though the trigger guess wasn't.
- **T1-D SHIPPED — a reset no longer wipes the graph.** `purgeAll()` was `MATCH (n) DETACH DELETE n` (every node of every label) with the narrow version commented out under a TODO. `SystemResetConfig` no longer calls it; the lineage is severed by `renewLineage()` and old nodes stay, told apart by their ON CREATE `lineageId`. `purgeAll()` survives as a deliberate wipe (test isolation, manual admin). Dead `GoalGraphService` dep removed from `SystemResetConfig`.
- **SEQUENCING WAS LOAD-BEARING, and it was paid before flipping the switch.** Keeping history makes previously-safe reads dangerous. `findAllDescriptions` + `findDescriptionsByStatus` feed goal dedup and the GIVEN_UP unachievable guard → both `lineageId`-scoped, else a dead lineage's abandoned goals block a fresh one's subgoals.
- **FIELD VERIFICATION (run `2026-07-26_19-58-56`, 55 cycles, PREFLIGHT: PASS) CAUGHT A DEFECT NO TEST COULD.** Method: seed a synthetic prior lineage, then boot (boot = reset = the operation that used to delete it). **First boot CRASHED**: `syncFromGraph()` → `goalGraphService.findAll()` was UNSCOPED, loaded another lineage's goals, and threw `UnsupportedOperationException` from `@PostConstruct` — the app would not start. I had classified `findAll()` as harmless "global" in the read audit; it is the most load-bearing cross-lineage read there is. Fixed + immutable-metadata path hardened. Second boot: reset ran, **prior lineage's 3 nodes SURVIVED** (1383 new-lineage nodes beside them); goal dedup saw 8 goals / **0** prior (unscoped: 9 / **1** leaking); GIVEN_UP guard saw **0** (unscoped: **1**).
- **T6-F SHIPPED** (`preserved_freshness.py` + 19 tests · `select_preserved.py --run-log` + 5): preflight branched on the mere EXISTENCE of `preserved-artifact/`, so a killed run scored a PREVIOUS run's artifact and skipped T6-A's reconstruction. A first attempt shipped and was a NO-OP (a resumed run prints its parent's cycle id in the T4-CD resume line; only the `CYCLE` banner means "this run ran it") — caught by checking against real logs, now a regression test. Reconstruction path fired in the field this evening (`preserved_source=reconstructed:cycle-1785090237357`); the STALE-copy branch is still unexercised (`t6f-stale-loop-copy-ignored` open).
- **T4-CJ slice 2 (partial but substantial):** write-once `createdInRunId` on goals (`runId` is deliberately LAST-WRITER per T1-FM, so it cannot answer "who created this") · `OrchestrationSnapshot.attemptRunId` · **a false-completion path closed** — `AutobuilderService`'s T2-CD criteria-less FULFILLED promotion could fire on a PREVIOUS run's score; now gated by `isThisRunsEvidence()` · prompt-side marking (`AttemptProvenance: EARLIER RUN of this lineage …`, fires only in that state, fails silent not false) · read surface classified: **41 reads, not the ~35 I quoted; only 4 were exposed AND live-consumed**; 3 scoped, and `findLatestEvaluationStatusForSubgoal` DELIBERATELY left lineage-wide because scoping it would REDUCE safety (it backs the #NoDiff divergence gate). Attribution completed at 4 write sites (`Neo4jScoreHistoryService` incl. its `%s` score-event labels, `SmsCycleIngestor`, `Neo4jMergeGraphService`).
- **T4-CL PROVEN, not inferred:** `mvn test` does NOT touch the production graph — canary node survived a full suite AND the `DETACH DELETE` graph test. It merely LOOKS broken because the pom points Testcontainers at Docker Desktop's socket while a hand-started Neo4j runs on the system socket, so the test container is invisible to `docker ps`. (The graph the user screenshotted was purged BY THE USER, not by a defect — I chased it as one first.)
- **Growth is a watch, not a subsystem:** one 4-run lineage ≈ 9,600 nodes → 100 lineages ≈ 1M, comfortable. Revisit past a few million nodes or if query latency shows in telemetry.
- **State:** suite **2126/0** · census 408 (13 open / 1 wip / 32 deferred / 366 closed) · warnings 0 · watch registry 6 · **37 commits unpushed, nothing pushed** · no JVM · synthetic test lineage cleaned up.
- **VERIFIED IN THE FIELD + TWO-LINEAGE FIXTURE EXISTS.** Run `2026-07-26_19-58-56` (55 cycles) proved T1-D: a real reset ran and the seeded prior lineage SURVIVED. Run `2026-07-26_21-33-16` then created a second lineage. **Final state in the live DB: `lineage-b21345d4` 2169 nodes + `lineage-f2b4593e` 1433 nodes, 0 crossing edges** — the first real fixture the cross-run merge chain has ever had. The FIRST boot attempt CRASHED (`syncFromGraph`→`findAll()` unscoped loaded another lineage's goals and threw from `@PostConstruct`); fixed + immutable-metadata path hardened. Second gap found by the USER looking at the browser: **787 of 2170 nodes (36%) had NO lineage stamp** — written by the `com.walpro:semantic-memory-substrate` DEPENDENCY, invisible to a write-site sweep of this repo. `SmsCycleIngestor` now stamps the episode + 2 hops post-commit; existing rows backfilled; DB reports zero unstamped.
- **WAVE 2 RESTRUCTURED — read [[project-wave2-structure]] FIRST for direction.** Through-line: *from disposable runs to an accumulating system*. Five layers: A substrate ✅ (tonight) · B portability · C reconciliation · D **consultation (thinnest, and the POINT)** · E yardsticks. Gate-driven sequence: measure overlap (`T6-G`) → ship ONE thin consumer → prove it helps (`T6-I`) → only then heavy build. **Five tickets filed across three tiers** (a first cut put them all in T6 — tier anchoring, corrected after user challenge): `T4-CT` (the loop CONSULTS accumulated knowledge — was homeless across T4-CJ slice 3 / T4-CI phase 3 / pattern P3-P4) · `T2-ER` (prior-lineage measurements: advisory, never binding — from tonight's contradiction where 3 reads had to be scoped out and a 4th deliberately left lineage-wide) · `T6-G` (cross-lineage overlap, gates T4-CN/CM/CS, decision rule allows CLOSING the chain on measurements) · `T6-H` (subtree-granularity outcome, blocks T4-CS) · `T6-I` (does accumulated knowledge actually help — the wave's falsifiability yardstick).
- **PROJECT PORTABILITY VISION FILED** (user, same session) — [[project-project-portability-vision]]: import/continue an existing project with or without its graph, store a project phase, restore it elsewhere, merge two lineages' code. `T4-CQ` / `T4-CR` / `T4-CS`. Unblocked by tonight's lineage separation; `T4-CR` also re-homes a residual left inside CLOSED T5-BD (`dump_graph.sh` is forensic, explicitly NOT reload-into-a-fresh-graph).

- **Standing principle earned tonight:** *a capability change that widens what the loop SEES must be preceded by scoping what it CONSULTS.* Keeping history was safe only because goal-dedup + the GIVEN_UP guard were scoped first.

Today's lineage built 10 correct-looking files with **no `@SpringBootApplication` class**. Two mechanisms should have caught it and neither did: `PackageGate` is only called from the completion/closure path (**0 invocations in 65 cycles** — the run halts first), and `PreservedArtifactService:95-96` defines `entryPointRequired` **retrospectively** as "some snapshot already has one", so it is false exactly in the lineages that need it — an entry-point-less tree was preserved as best (`entryPointPresent=false, entryPointRequired=false, verified=COMPILE_OK`) and inherited by the resumed child. **The loop has no forward-looking requirement for a runnable entry point anywhere.** Corrected mid-session by run 3: the condition is **costly, not terminal** (the loop wrote `LibraryApplication.java` ~12 min in and scored 9/23) — the damage is 2 runs + ~45 min and a dead inherited state. Fix shape: derive the requirement from the root goal/spec, feed the ONE derivation into both existing consumers.
- **T5-BH's `ReplanPatternDetector` question is ANSWERED — result DELETE + HARVEST (not executed; needs a green suite).** First, **the liveness instrument shipped yesterday could not emit**: it logged at `log.debug` while `logback-spring.xml:48` pins `com.walpro.autobuilder` to INFO. Proven in the field, not inferred — run1 (old jar) 32 cycles / **0 lines**, run2 (fixed) 65 cycles / **65 lines**. **All 183 `log.debug` calls in `src/main` are unreachable in every run** → rule now in [[project-log-and-backup-conventions]]: a field instrument logs at INFO or emits a behaviour event, never debug.
